    <script>
        const api_cog = "/research/cog/api/cog/";
        $(document).ready(function () {
            $('#organism_dialog_button').click(function () {
                let modal = document.getElementById('snp-popup');
                modal.style.display = "none";
            });

            $("tr").click(function () {
                const cog = $('#cog_container').attr("data");
                const taxid = $(this).attr("data");
                $.get(api_cog + "?taxid=" + taxid + '&cog=' + cog, function (data) {
                    console.log(data);
                    if (data.count > 0) {
                        let modal = document.getElementById('snp-popup');
                        let organism = data.results[0].organism;
                        $('#organism_name').text(organism.genome_name);
                        $('#organism_assembly').text(organism.assembly_id);
                        $('#organism_assembly').attr("href", "https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/assembly/" + organism.assembly_id);
                        $('#organism_taxid').text(organism.taxid);
                        $('#organism_taxid').attr("href", "https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/Taxonomy/Browser/wwwtax.cgi?id=" + organism.taxid);
                        $('#organism_category').text(organism.category.name);
                        $('#organism_category').attr("href", "https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/Taxonomy/Browser/wwwtax.cgi?id=" + organism.category.taxid);

                        $('#cog_table_body').html("");
                        data.results.forEach(function (item, index, arr) {
                            let row = $('<tr/>');
                            row.append($('<td/>').text(item.gene_tag));
                            let protein_link = $('<a/>');
                            protein_link.attr("href", "https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/protein/" + item.protein.name);
                            protein_link.attr("target", "_blank");
                            protein_link.text(item.protein.name);
                            row.append($('<td/>').append(protein_link));
                            row.append($('<td/>').text(item.protein.len));
                            row.append($('<td/>').text(item.footprint));
                            row.append($('<td/>').text(item.footprint_length));
                            row.append($('<td/>').text(item.bitscore));
                            row.append($('<td/>').text(Number.parseFloat(item.evalue).toExponential()));
                            row.append($('<td/>').text(item.profile_length));
                            row.append($('<td/>').text(item.prot_coord));
                            row.append($('<td/>').text(item.membership.membership_class));
                            $('#cog_table_body').append(row);
                        });
                        modal.style.display = "block";
                        window.onclick = function (event) {
                            if (event.target == modal) {
                                modal.style.display = "none";
                            }
                        };
                    }

                });
            });
        });

    </script>
